<chrono>\n#include <climits>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Cholesky decomposition (blocked right-looking algorithm, CUDA)\n// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular\n//\n// The matrix is processed in NB x NB column blocks. For every block k:\n//   1. POTRF  : factorize the diagonal block (single CUDA block, shared memory)\n//   2. TRSM   : solve the panel below the diagonal block against L_kk^T\n//   3. SYRK   : rank-NB update of the remaining trailing submatrix (lower part)\n// All three steps are data parallel across the (large) trailing submatrix, which\n// also carries the bulk of the O(n^3/3) work.\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                       \\\n    do {                                                                                       \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                       \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                             \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);  \\\n            exit(1);                                                                           \\\n        }                                                                                      \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Blocking parameters. TILE must match NB so that a single trailing-update tile\n// covers exactly one block column of the panel.\nstatic constexpr int NB = 64;         // algorithmic block size\nstatic constexpr int TILE = 64;       // GEMM tile (rows/cols per CUDA block)\nstatic constexpr int BK = 16;         // GEMM tile depth\nstatic constexpr int GEMM_THREADS = 256;\nstatic constexpr int TRSM_ROWS = 32;  // panel rows handled by one TRSM block\n\n// C = alpha * A * B^T + beta * C   (all matrices row-major)\n// Each thread owns a 4x4 register tile with a strided (stride 16) mapping, which\n// keeps both the shared memory reads and the global stores conflict free.\n// Accumulation runs over k in ascending order with a single accumulator per\n// output element, i.e. the same summation order as the sequential reference.\n// If lowerOnly is set, only elements with j <= i are computed/stored.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(GEMM_THREADS) void gemmNT(const int M, const int N, const int K,\n                                                       const double alpha,\n                                                       const double* __restrict__ A, const int lda,\n                                                       const double* __restrict__ B, const int ldb,\n                                                       const double beta, double* __restrict__ C,\n                                                       const int ldc, const bool lowerOnly) {\n    const int bi = blockIdx.y * TILE;\n    const int bj = blockIdx.x * TILE;\n    if (lowerOnly && bj > bi) {\n        return;  // tile lies strictly in the upper triangle\n    }\n\n    __shared__ double As[BK][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][TILE + 1];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int tx = tid & 15;   // column direction of the register tile\n    const int ty = tid >> 4;   // row direction of the register tile\n    const int ll = tid & 15;   // k index used while loading\n    const int lr = tid >> 4;   // row index used while loading\n\n    double acc[4][4];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int q = 0; q < 4; ++q) {\n            acc[p][q] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (int k0 = 0; k0 < K; k0 += BK) {\n        const int gc = k0 + ll;\n        const bool kValid = gc < K;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int u = 0; u < 4; ++u) {\n            const int r = lr + 16 * u;\n            const int gra = bi + r;\n            const int grb = bj + r;\n            As[ll][r] = (kValid && gra < M) ? A[(size_t)gra * lda + gc] : 0.0;\n            Bs[ll][r] = (kValid && grb < N) ? B[(size_t)grb * ldb + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int l = 0; l < BK; ++l) {\n            double a[4], b[4];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int e = 0; e < 4; ++e) {\n                a[e] = As[l][ty + 16 * e];\n                b[e] = Bs[l][tx + 16 * e];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int q = 0; q < 4; ++q) {\n                    acc[p][q] += a[p] * b[q];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {\n        const int i = bi + ty + 16 * p;\n        if (i >= M) {\n            continue;\n        }\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int q = 0; q < 4; ++q) {\n            const int j = bj + tx + 16 * q;\n            if (j >= N || (lowerOnly && j > i)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            double* dst = &C[(size_t)i * ldc + j];\n            *dst = (beta == 0.0) ? alpha * acc[p][q] : beta * (*dst) + alpha * acc[p][q];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky of the nb x nb diagonal block at (k, k), executed by a\n// single CUDA block entirely in shared memory (right-looking).\n// Additionally inverts the resulting triangular factor into Linv, which turns\n// the subsequent panel solve into a plain (fully parallel) GEMM.\n// The inverse is kept in the otherwise unused strictly-upper half of the shared\n// tile in transposed form: inv[r][c] (r > c) lives in s[c][r].\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(256) void potrfDiag(double* __restrict__ A, const size_t n, const int k,\n                                                 const int nb, double* __restrict__ Linv,\n                                                 int* __restrict__ info) {\n    __shared__ double s[NB][NB + 1];\n    __shared__ double idiag[NB];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int nthreads = blockDim.x;\n\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += nthreads) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx - r * nb;\n        s[r][c] = (c <= r) ? A[(size_t)(k + r) * n + (k + c)] : 0.0;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int j = 0; j < nb; ++j) {\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const double val = s[j][j];\n            if (!(val > 0.0)) {\n                atomicMin(info, k + j);\n            }\n            s[j][j] = sqrt(val);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const double d = s[j][j];\n        for (int i = j + 1 + tid; i < nb; i += nthreads) {\n            s[i][j] /= d;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        // rank-1 update of the trailing lower part of the block\n        const int m = nb - j - 1;\n        for (int idx = tid; idx < m * m; idx += nthreads) {\n            const int r = j + 1 + idx / m;\n            const int c = j + 1 + idx % m;\n            if (r >= c) {\n                s[r][c] -= s[r][j] * s[c][j];\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Store the factor before the strictly-upper half gets used for the inverse\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += nthreads) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx - r * nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            A[(size_t)(k + r) * n + (k + c)] = s[r][c];\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Invert the lower triangular factor: one thread per column, forward\n    // substitution on L * X = I. Every thread only writes its own column of X,\n    // so no synchronization is needed inside the loop.\n    if (tid < nb) {\n        const int c = tid;\n        const double dc = 1.0 / s[c][c];\n        idiag[c] = dc;\n        double prev = dc;  // inv[c][c]\n        for (int i = c + 1; i < nb; ++i) {\n            double sum = s[i][c] * dc;\n            for (int l = c + 1; l < i; ++l) {\n                sum += s[i][l] * s[c][l];\n            }\n            prev = -sum / s[i][i];\n            s[c][i] = prev;\n        }\n        (void)prev;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += nthreads) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx - r * nb;\n        Linv[idx] = (c > r) ? 0.0 : ((c == r) ? idiag[r] : s[c][r]);\n    }\n}\n\n// Zero the strictly upper triangular part of the block rows [k, k + nb).\n__global__ void zeroUpperRows(double* __restrict__ A, const size_t n, const int k, const int nb) {\n    const size_t idx = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t width = n - k;  // columns k .. n-1 hold the part that can be non-zero\n    if (idx >= (size_t)nb * width) {\n        return;\n    }\n    const size_t r = idx / width;\n    const size_t c = idx - r * width;  // relative to column k\n    if (c > r) {\n        A[(size_t)(k + r) * n + (k + c)] = 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void addDiagonal(double* __restrict__ A, const size_t n, const double v) {\n    const size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < n) {\n        A[i * n + i] += v;\n    }\n}\n\nstatic inline unsigned int ceilDiv(const size_t a, const unsigned int b) {\n    return (unsigned int)((a + b - 1) / b);\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n    int* dInfo = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, n * n * generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Compute A = B * B^T on the GPU\n    double* dB = nullptr;\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n Every thread only writes its own column of X,\n    // so no synchronization is needed inside the loop.\n    if (tid < nb) {\n        const int c = tid;\n        const double dc = 1.0 / s[c][c];\n      sum += s[i][l] * s[c][l];\n            }\n            prev = -sum / s[i][i];\n            s[c][i] = prev;\n        }\n        (void)prev;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int idx = tid; addDiagonal(double* __restrict__ A, const size_t n, const double v) {\n    const size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < n) {\n        A[i * n + i] += v;\n    }\n}\n\nstatic inline unsigned int ceilDiv(const size_t a, const unsigned int b) {\n    return (unsigned int)((a + b - 1) / b);\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n    double* dLinv = nullptr;\n    int* dInfo = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dLinv, (size_t)NB * NB * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dInfo, sizeof(int)));\n\n    cudaStream_t sMain, sCopy;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&sMain));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&sCopy));\n    cudaEvent_t rowsReady;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&rowsReady, A.data(), n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, sMain));\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += NB) {\n        const int nb = (int)std::min((size_t)NB, n - k);\n        const size_t rows = n - (k + nb);\n\n        // 1. factorize the diagonal block (and invert it)\n        potrfDiag<<<1, 256, 0, sMain>>>(dA, n, (int)k, nb, dLinv, dInfo);\n\n        // The block rows [k, k+nb) are final nb);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(rowsReady, sMain));\n\n        if (rows > 0) {\n            // 2. panel solve, expressed as P = P * Linv^T (in-place: every block\n            //    reads exactly the rows it later writes, separated by a barrier)\n            double* panel = dA + (k + nb) * n + k;\n            gemmNT<<<dim3(1, ceilDiv(rows, TILE)), GEMM_THREADS, 0, sMain>>>(\n                (int)rows, nb, nb, 1.0, panel, (int)n, dLinv, NB, 0.0, panel, (int)n, false);\n\n            // 3. trailing update: A22 -= P * P^T  (lower triangle only)\n            double* trailing = dA + (k + true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // For every block k:\n//   1. POTRF  : factorize the diagonal block (single CUDA block, shared memory)\n//   2. TRSM   : solve the panel below the diagonal block against L_kk^T\n//   3. SYRK   : rank-NB update of the remaining trailing submatrix (lower part)\n// All three steps are data parallel across the (large) trailing submatrix, which\n// also carries the bulk of the O(n^3/3) work.\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                       \\\n    do {                                                                                       \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                       \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                             \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);  \\\n            exit(1);                                                                           \\\n        }                                                                                      \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Blocking parameters. TILE must match NB so that a single trailing-update tile\n// covers exactly one block column of the panel.\nstatic constexpr int NB = 64;         // algorithmic block size\nstatic constexpr int TILE = 64;       // GEMM tile (rows/cols per CUDA block)\nstatic constexpr int BK = 16;         // GEMM tile depth\nstatic constexpr int GEMM_THREADS = 256;\n\n// C = alpha * A * B^T + beta * C   (all matrices row-major)\n// Each thread owns a 4x4 register tile with a strided (stride 16) mapping, which\n// keeps both the shared memory reads and the global stores conflict free.\n// Accumulation runs over k in ascending order with a single accumulator per\n// output element, i.e. the same summation order as the sequential reference.\n// If lowerOnly is set, only elements with j <= i are computed/stored.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(GEMM_THREADS) void gemmNT(const int M, const int N, const int K,\n                                                       const double alpha,\n                                                       const double* __restrict__ A, const int lda,\n                                                       const double* __restrict__ B, const int ldb,\n                                                       const double beta, double* __restrict__ C,\n                                                       const int ldc, const bool lowerOnly) {\n    const int bi = blockIdx.y * TILE;\n    const int bj = blockIdx.x * TILE;\n    if (lowerOnly && bj > bi) {\n        return;  // tile lies strictly in the upper triangle\n    }\n\n    __shared__ double As[BK][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][TILE + 1];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int tx = tid & 15;   // column direction of the register tile\n    const int ty = tid >> 4;   // row direction of the register tile\n    const int ll = tid & 15;   // k index used while loading\n    const int lr = tid >> 4;   // row index used while loading\n\n    double acc[4][4];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int q = 0; q < 4; ++q) {\n            acc[p][q] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (int k0 = 0; k0 < K; k0 += BK) {\n        const int gc = k0 + ll;\n        const bool kValid = gc < K;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int u = 0; u < 4; ++u) {\n            const int r = lr + 16 * u;\n            const int gra = bi + r;\n            const int grb = bj + r;\n            As[ll][r] = (kValid && gra < M) ? A[(size_t)gra * lda + gc] : 0.0;\n            Bs[ll][r] = (kValid && grb < N) ? B[(size_t)grb * ldb + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int l = 0; l < BK; ++l) {\n            double a[4], b[4];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int e = 0; e < 4; ++e) {\n                a[e] = As[l][ty + 16 * e];\n                b[e] = Bs[l][tx + 16 * e];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int q = 0; q < 4; ++q) {\n                    acc[p][q] += a[p] * b[q];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {\n        const int i = bi + ty + 16 * p;\n        if (i >= M) {\n            continue;\n        }\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int q = 0; q < 4; ++q) {\n            const int j = bj + tx + 16 * q;\n            if (j >= N || (lowerOnly && j > i)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            double* dst = &C[(size_t)i * ldc + j];\n            *dst = (beta == 0.0) ? alpha * acc[p][q] : beta * (*dst) + alpha * acc[p][q];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky of the nb x nb diagonal block at (k, k), executed by a\n// single CUDA block entirely in shared memory (right-looking).\n// Additionally inverts the resulting triangular factor into Linv, which turns\n// the subsequent panel solve into a plain (fully parallel) GEMM.\n// The inverse is kept in the otherwise unused strictly-upper half of the shared\n// tile in transposed form: inv[r][c] (r > c) lives in s[c][r].\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(256) void potrfDiag(double* __restrict__ A, const size_t n, const int k,\n                                                 const int nb, double* __restrict__ Linv,\n                                                 int* __restrict__ info) {\n    __shared__ double s[NB][NB + 1];\n    __shared__ double idiag[NB];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int nthreads = blockDim.x;\n\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += nthreads) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx - r * nb;\n        s[r][c] = (c <= r) ? A[(size_t)(k + r) * n + (k + c)] : 0.0;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int j = 0; j < nb; ++j) {\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const double val = s[j][j];\n            if (!(val > 0.0)) {\n                atomicMin(info, k + j);\n            }\n            s[j][j] = sqrt(val);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const double d = s[j][j];\n        for (int i = j + 1 + tid; i < nb; i += nthreads) {\n            s[i][j] /= d;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        // rank-1 update of the trailing lower part of the block\n        const int m = nb - j - 1;\n        for (int idx = tid; idx < m * m; idx += nthreads) {\n            const int r = j + 1 + idx / m;\n            const int c = j + 1 + idx % m;\n            if (r >= c) {\n                s[r][c] -= s[r][j] * s[c][j];\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Store the factor before the strictly-upper half gets used for the inverse\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += nthreads) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx - r * nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            A[(size_t)(k + r) * n + (k + c)] = s[r][c];\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Invert the lower triangular factor: one thread per column, forward\n    // substitution on L * X = I. Every thread only writes its own column of X,\n    // so no synchronization is needed inside the loop.\n    if (tid < nb) {\n        const int c = tid;\n        const double dc = 1.0 / s[c][c];\n        idiag[c] = dc;\n        double prev = dc;  // inv[c][c]\n        for (int i = c + 1; i < nb; ++i) {\n            double sum = s[i][c] * dc;\n            for (int l = c + 1; l < i; ++l) {\n                sum += s[i][l] * s[c][l];\n            }\n            prev = -sum / s[i][i];\n            s[c][i] = prev;\n        }\n        (void)prev;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += nthreads) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx - r * nb;\n        Linv[r * NB + c] = (c > r) ? 0.0 : ((c == r) ? idiag[r] : s[c][r]);\n    }\n}\n\n// Zero the strictly upper triangular part of the block rows [k, k + nb).\n__global__ void zeroUpperRows(double* __restrict__ A, const size_t n, const int k, const int nb) {\n    const size_t idx = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t width = n - k;  // columns k .. n-1 hold the part that can be non-zero\n    if (idx >= (size_t)nb * width) {\n        return;\n    }\n    const size_t r = idx / width;\n    const size_t c = idx - r * width;  // relative to column k\n    if (c > r) {\n        A[(size_t)(k + r) * n + (k + c)] = 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void addDiagonal(double* __restrict__ A, const size_t n, const double v) {\n    const size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < n) {\n        A[i * n + i] += v;\n    }\n}\n\nstatic inline unsigned int ceilDiv(const size_t a, const unsigned int b) {\n    return (unsigned int)((a + b - 1) / b);\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n    double* dLinv = nullptr;\n    int* dInfo = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dLinv, (size_t)NB * NB * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dInfo, sizeof(int)));\n\n    cudaStream_t sMain, sCopy;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&sMain));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&sCopy));\n    cudaEvent_t rowsReady;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&rowsReady, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n\n    const int infoInit = INT_MAX;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dInfo, &infoInit, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, sMain));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dA, A.data(), n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, sMain));\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += NB) {\n        const int nb = (int)std::min((size_t)NB, n - k);\n        const size_t rows = n - (k + nb);\n\n        // 1. factorize the diagonal block (and invert it)\n        potrfDiag<<<1, 256, 0, sMain>>>(dA, n, (int)k, nb, dLinv, dInfo);\n\n        // The block rows [k, k+nb) are final now; zero their upper part and\n        // stream them back to the host while the trailing update runs.\n        zeroUpperRows<<<ceilDiv((size_t)nb * (n - k), 256), 256, 0, sMain>>>(dA, n, (int)k, nb);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(rowsReady, sMain));\n\n        if (rows > 0) {\n            // 2. panel solve, expressed as P = P * Linv^T (in-place: every block\n            //    reads exactly the rows it later writes, separated by a barrier)\n            double* panel = dA + (k + nb) * n + k;\n            gemmNT<<<dim3(1, ceilDiv(rows, TILE)), GEMM_THREADS, 0, sMain>>>(\n                (int)rows, nb, nb, 1.0, panel, (int)n, dLinv, NB, 0.0, panel, (int)n, false);\n\n            // 3. trailing update: A22 -= P * P^T  (lower triangle only)\n            double* trailing = dA + (k + nb) * n + (k + nb);\n            const unsigned int tiles = ceilDiv(rows, TILE);\n            gemmNT<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), GEMM_THREADS, 0, sMain>>>(\n                (int)rows, (int)rows, nb, -1.0, panel, (int)n, panel, (int)n, 1.0, trailing,\n                (int)n, true);\n        }\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(sCopy, rowsReady, 0));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data() + k * n, dA + k * n, (size_t)nb * n * sizeof(double),\n                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, sCopy));\n    }\n\n    int info = INT_MAX;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&info, dInfo, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, sMain));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(sMain));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(sCopy));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(rowsReady));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(sMain));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(sCopy));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dLinv));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dInfo));\n\n    if (info != INT_MAX) {\n        // Matrix is not positive definite\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\\n\", info);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n\n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n\n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Compute A = B * B^T on the GPU\n    double* dB = nullptr;\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dB, B.data(), n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    const unsigned int tiles = ceilDiv(n, TILE);\n    gemmNT<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), GEMM_THREADS>>>((int)n, (int)n, (int)n, 1.0, dB, (int)n, dB,\n                                                 (int)n, 0.0, dA, (int)n, false);\n\n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    addDiagonal<<<ceilDiv(n, 256), 256>>>(dA, n, (double)n);\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), dA, n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n\n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n\n    // Compute L * L^T on the GPU\n    double* dL = nullptr;\n    double* dR = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dL, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dR, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dL, L.data(), n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    const unsigned int tiles = ceilDiv(n, TILE);\n    gemmNT<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), GEMM_THREADS>>>((int)n, (int)n, (int)n, 1.0, dL, (int)n, dL,\n                                                 (int)n, 0.0, dR, (int)n, false);\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(reconstructed.data(), dR, n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dL));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dR));\n\n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n\n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n\n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", n, n);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate matrix\n    std::vector<double> A(n * n);\n    std::vector<double> A_orig;\n\n    // Generate positive definite matrix\n    printf(\"Generating positive definite matrix...\\n\");\n    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);\n\n    if (validate) {\n        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation\n    }\n\n    // Perform Cholesky decomposition\n    printf(\"Computing Cholesky decomposition...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (!success) {\n        printf(\"Cholesky decomposition failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)\n    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(A, \"CholeskyL\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":16,"oldLines":10,"newStart":16,"newLines":13,"lines":[" //"," // The matrix is processed in NB x NB column blocks. k);\n        const size_t rows = n - (k + nb);\n\n        // 1. factorize the diagonal block (and invert it)\n        potrfDiag<<<1, 256, 0, sMain>>>(dA, n, (int)k, nb, dLinv, dInfo);\n\n        // The block rows [k, k+nb) are final now; zero their upper part and\n        // stream them back to the host while the trailing update runs.\n        zeroUpperRows<<<ceilDiv((size_t)nb * (n - For every block k:\n//   1. POTRF  : factorize the diagonal block (single CUDA block, shared memory)\n//               and invert the resulting triangular factor\n//   2. TRSM   : solve the panel below the diagonal block, expressed as the GEMM\n//               P = P * L_kk^-T so that it parallelizes over all panel rows\n//   3. SYRK   : rank-NB update of the remaining trailing submatrix (lower part)\n// Step 3 carries the bulk of the O(n^3/3) work and is parallel over the whole\n// trailing submatrix. Finished block rows are streamed back to the host on a\n// second stream, overlapping the device-to-host transfer with the computation.\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                       \\\n    do {                                                                                       \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                       \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                             \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);  \\\n            exit(1);                                                                           \\\n        }                                                                                      \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Blocking parameters. TILE must match NB so that a single trailing-update tile\n// covers exactly one block column of the panel.\nstatic constexpr int NB = 64;         // algorithmic block size\nstatic constexpr int TILE = 64;       // GEMM tile (rows/cols per CUDA block)\nstatic constexpr int BK = 16;         // GEMM tile depth\nstatic constexpr int GEMM_THREADS = 256;\n\n// C = alpha * A * B^T + beta * C   (all matrices row-major)\n// Each thread owns a 4x4 register tile with a strided (stride 16) mapping, which\n// keeps both the shared memory reads and the global stores conflict free.\n// Accumulation runs over k in ascending order with a single accumulator per\n// output element, i.e. the same summation order as the sequential reference.\n// If lowerOnly is set, only elements with j <= i are computed/stored.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(GEMM_THREADS) void gemmNT(const int M, const int N, const int K,\n                                                       const double alpha,\n                                                       const double* A, const int lda,\n                                                       const double* B, const int ldb,\n                                                       const double beta, double* C,\n                                                       const int ldc, const bool lowerOnly) {\n    const int bi = blockIdx.y * TILE;\n    const int bj = blockIdx.x * TILE;\n    if (lowerOnly && bj > bi) {\n        return;  // tile lies strictly in the upper triangle\n    }\n\n    __shared__ double As[BK][TILE + 1];\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][TILE + 1];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int tx = tid & 15;   // column direction of the register tile\n    const int ty = tid >> 4;   // row direction of the register tile\n    const int ll = tid & 15;   // k index used while loading\n    const int lr = tid >> 4;   // row index used while loading\n\n    double acc[4][4];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int q = 0; q < 4; ++q) {\n            acc[p][q] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (int k0 = 0; k0 < K; k0 += BK) {\n        const int gc = k0 + ll;\n        const bool kValid = gc < K;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int u = 0; u < 4; ++u) {\n            const int r = lr + 16 * u;\n            const int gra = bi + r;\n            const int grb = bj + r;\n            As[ll][r] = (kValid && gra < M) ? A[(size_t)gra * lda + gc] : 0.0;\n            Bs[ll][r] = (kValid && grb < N) ? B[(size_t)grb * ldb + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int l = 0; l < BK; ++l) {\n            double a[4], b[4];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int e = 0; e < 4; ++e) {\n                a[e] = As[l][ty + 16 * e];\n                b[e] = Bs[l][tx + 16 * e];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int q = 0; q < 4; ++q) {\n                    acc[p][q] += a[p] * b[q];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int p = 0; p < 4; ++p) {\n        const int i = bi + ty + 16 * p;\n        if (i >= M) {\n            continue;\n        }\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int q = 0; q < 4; ++q) {\n            const int j = bj + tx + 16 * q;\n            if (j >= N || (lowerOnly && j > i)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            double* dst = &C[(size_t)i * ldc + j];\n            *dst = (beta == 0.0) ? alpha * acc[p][q] : beta * (*dst) + alpha * acc[p][q];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky of the nb x nb diagonal block at (k, k), executed by a\n// single CUDA block entirely in shared memory (right-looking).\n// Additionally inverts the resulting triangular factor into Linv, which turns\n// the subsequent panel solve into a plain (fully parallel) GEMM.\n// The inverse is kept in the otherwise unused strictly-upper half of the shared\n// tile in transposed form: inv[r][c] (r > c) lives in s[c][r].\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(256) void potrfDiag(double* __restrict__ A, const size_t n, const int k,\n                                                 const int nb, double* __restrict__ Linv,\n                                                 int* __restrict__ info) {\n    __shared__ double s[NB][NB + 1];\n    __shared__ double idiag[NB];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int nthreads = blockDim.x;\n\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += nthreads) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx - r * nb;\n        s[r][c] = (c <= r) ? A[(size_t)(k + r) * n + (k + c)] : 0.0;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int j = 0; j < nb; ++j) {\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const double val = s[j][j];\n            if (!(val > 0.0)) {\n                atomicMin(info, k + j);\n            }\n            s[j][j] = sqrt(val);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const double d = s[j][j];\n        for (int i = j + 1 + tid; i < nb; i += nthreads) {\n            s[i][j] /= d;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        // rank-1 update of the trailing lower part of the block\n        const int m = nb - j - 1;\n        for (int idx = tid; idx < m * m; idx += nthreads) {\n            const int r = j + 1 + idx / m;\n            const int c = j + 1 + idx % m;\n            if (r >= c) {\n                s[r][c] -= s[r][j] * s[c][j];\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Store the factor before the strictly-upper half gets used for the inverse\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += nthreads) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx - r * nb;\n        if (c <= r) {\n            A[(size_t)(k + r) * n + (k + c)] = s[r][c];\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Invert the lower triangular factor: one thread per column, forward\n    // substitution on L * X = I. Every thread only writes its own column of X,\n    // so no synchronization is needed inside the loop.\n    if (tid < nb) {\n        const int c = tid;\n        const double dc = 1.0 / s[c][c];\n        idiag[c] = dc;\n        double prev = dc;  // inv[c][c]\n        for (int i = c + 1; i < nb; ++i) {\n            double sum = s[i][c] * dc;\n            for (int l = c + 1; l < i; ++l) {\n                sum += s[i][l] * s[c][l];\n            }\n            prev = -sum / s[i][i];\n            s[c][i] = prev;\n        }\n        (void)prev;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < nb * nb; idx += nthreads) {\n        const int r = idx / nb;\n        const int c = idx - r * nb;\n        Linv[r * NB + c] = (c > r) ? 0.0 : ((c == r) ? idiag[r] : s[c][r]);\n    }\n}\n\n// Zero the strictly upper triangular part of the block rows [k, k + nb).\n__global__ void zeroUpperRows(double* __restrict__ A, const size_t n, const int k, const int nb) {\n    const size_t idx = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t width = n - k;  // columns k .. n-1 hold the part that can be non-zero\n    if (idx >= (size_t)nb * width) {\n        return;\n    }\n    const size_t r = idx / width;\n    const size_t c = idx - r * width;  // relative to column k\n    if (c > r) {\n        A[(size_t)(k + r) * n + (k + c)] = 0.0;\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void addDiagonal(double* __restrict__ A, const size_t n, const double v) {\n    const size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < n) {\n        A[i * n + i] += v;\n    }\n}\n\nstatic inline unsigned int ceilDiv(const size_t a, const unsigned int b) {\n    return (unsigned int)((a + b - 1) / b);\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n    double* dLinv = nullptr;\n    int* dInfo = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dLinv, (size_t)NB * NB * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dInfo, sizeof(int)));\n\n    cudaStream_t sMain, sCopy;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&sMain));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&sCopy));\n    cudaEvent_t rowsReady;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&rowsReady, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n\n    const int infoInit = INT_MAX;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dInfo, &infoInit, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, sMain));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dA, A.data(), n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, sMain));\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += NB) {\n        const int nb = (int)std::min((size_t)NB, n - k);\n        const size_t rows = n - (k + nb);\n\n        // 1. factorize the diagonal block (and invert it)\n        potrfDiag<<<1, 256, 0, sMain>>>(dA, n, (int)k, nb, dLinv, dInfo);\n\n        // The block rows [k, k+nb) are final now; zero their upper part and\n        // stream them back to the host while the trailing update runs.\n        zeroUpperRows<<<ceilDiv((size_t)nb * (n - k), 256), 256, 0, sMain>>>(dA, n, (int)k, nb);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(rowsReady, sMain));\n\n        if (rows > 0) {\n            // 2. panel solve, expressed as P = P * Linv^T (in-place: every block\n            //    reads exactly the rows it later writes, separated by a barrier)\n            double* panel = dA + (k + nb) * n + k;\n            gemmNT<<<dim3(1, ceilDiv(rows, TILE)), GEMM_THREADS, 0, sMain>>>(\n                (int)rows, nb, nb, 1.0, panel, (int)n, dLinv, NB, 0.0, panel, (int)n, false);\n\n            // 3. trailing update: A22 -= P * P^T  (lower triangle only)\n            double* trailing = dA + (k + nb) * n + (k + nb);\n            const unsigned int tiles = ceilDiv(rows, TILE);\n            gemmNT<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), GEMM_THREADS, 0, sMain>>>(\n                (int)rows, (int)rows, nb, -1.0, panel, (int)n, panel, (int)n, 1.0, trailing,\n                (int)n, true);\n        }\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(sCopy, rowsReady, 0));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data() + k * n, dA + k * n, (size_t)nb * n * sizeof(double),\n                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, sCopy));\n    }\n\n    int info = INT_MAX;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&info, dInfo, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, sMain));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(sMain));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(sCopy));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(rowsReady));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(sMain));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(sCopy));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dLinv));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dInfo));\n\n    if (info != INT_MAX) {\n        // Matrix is not positive definite\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\\n\", info);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n\n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n\n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Compute A = B * B^T on the GPU\n    double* dB = nullptr;\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dB, B.data(), n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    const unsigned int tiles = ceilDiv(n, TILE);\n    gemmNT<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), GEMM_THREADS>>>((int)n, (int)n, (int)n, 1.0, dB, (int)n, dB,\n                                                 (int)n, 0.0, dA, (int)n, false);\n\n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    addDiagonal<<<ceilDiv(n, 256), 256>>>(dA, n, (double)n);\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), dA, n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n\n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n\n    // Compute L * L^T on the GPU\n    double* dL = nullptr;\n    double* dR = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dL, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dR, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dL, L.data(), n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    const unsigned int tiles = ceilDiv(n, TILE);\n    gemmNT<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), GEMM_THREADS>>>((int)n, (int)n, (int)n, 1.0, dL, (int)n, dL,\n                                                 (int)n, 0.0, dR, (int)n, false);\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(reconstructed.data(), dR, n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dL));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dR));\n\n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n\n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n\n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num> {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeoSKudoihzmbY1pj9Jpr","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The benchmark now runs a blocked, right-looking Cholesky entirely on the GPU.\n\n## What changed\n\n**`cholesky/cholesky.cpp`** — all three original functions are now CUDA-parallel:\n\n- **`choleskyDecomposition`** — blocked right-looking factorization with `NB = 64`:\n  1. `potrfDiag` factorizes the 64×64 diagonal block in shared memory (one CUDA block) and *also* inverts the resulting triangular factor, keeping the inverse in the otherwise-unused strictly-upper half of the shared tile.\n  2. The panel solve below the diagonal block becomes `P = P · L_kk⁻ᵀ`, i.e. a plain GEMM that parallelizes over all panel rows instead of a row-sequential triangular solve (this alone was worth ~15%).\n  3. The trailing update `A₂₂ -= P·Pᵀ` (lower triangle only; upper tiles exit immediately) carries the bulk of the n³/3 work.\n- **`gemmNT`** — the one tuned kernel used everywhere: 64×64×16 tiling, 256 threads, 4×4 register tile with a stride-16 thread mapping so shared reads and global stores are bank/coalescing clean. It hits **548 GFLOPS on the standalone n=3000 DGEMM**, essentially the RTX 3090's FP64 peak (0.556 TFLOPS).\n- Finished block rows are zeroed above the diagonal and streamed back to the host on a second stream, overlapping the D2H transfer with the trailing update.\n- Matrix generation (`B·Bᵀ`) and validation (`L·Lᵀ`) also run on the GPU via the same kernel; the random generation stays on the host so the input matrix is unchanged.\n- Positive-definiteness detection is kept: an `atomicMin` device flag records the first failing diagonal index and the same message/exit path is produced (verified by forcing a non-PD matrix).\n\n**`cholesky/CMakeLists.txt`** — enables the CUDA language, compiles `cholesky.cpp` with nvcc (`LANGUAGE CUDA`), `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native`, host flags passed through `-Xcompiler`. Same executable name, no new files, no external dependencies.\n\n## Results (RTX 3090, one GPU)\n\n| n | sequential | CUDA | speedup |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 4096 | 18058 ms (3.8 GFLOPS) | 71 ms (323 GFLOPS) | ~254× |\n| 8192 | — | 389 ms (471 GFLOPS) | — |\n\nTimings include all H2D/D2H transfers and device allocations, so the reported GFLOPS understate kernel throughput (at n=4096 the unavoidable H2D of 128 MB is ~11 ms of the 71 ms).\n\nValidation passes for every size tested (1, 2, 3, 17, 64, 65, 127, 128, 200, 512, 513, 1000, 2048, 3000, 4096, 8192). `-r` statistics agree with the sequential reference to 15–16 significant digits (Sum bit-identical at n=512); the FNV hash necessarily differs, since any parallel reassociation changes the last bits. Max absolute error is ~4× the sequential algorithm's (blocked summation order), far inside the 1e-6 tolerance. `compute-sanitizer` memcheck/racecheck/synccheck/initcheck all report 0 errors.\n\nOne thing I did not do: the box has 4 GPUs and this uses one. Multi-GPU Cholesky needs a distributed 2D block-cyclic layout plus inter-GPU panel broadcasts — a much larger change than the requested parallelization, and at these sizes PCIe transfer would eat most of the gain."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":798,"cache_read_input_tokens":114221,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":798},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7d28c332-64d0-4d87-b5e6-32c65b6e2876","uuid":"8091ec99-702c-407b-a99f-05aa4c0be856","timestamp":"2026-09-07T04:20:44.071Z","request_id":"req_011CeoSKtaZAoknBUiERAYy7"}
